Result: 在多年份SEC财务披露语料测试该方法，可准确捕捉波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断、邦吉收购Viterra等重大公司事件的独特、可解释语义特征，并能精确溯源到产生这些特征的原始语句。

Conclusion: 该方法无需领域特定训练或大模型推理，能够高效、解释性强地揭示财务披露文档中的重要企业动态，为企业情报分析提供了实用新工具。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [119] [Structured Transforms for Low-Overhead Quantization of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11687)
*Daria Cherniuk,Alexander Rudikov,Boris Kashin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kashin分解的权重量化新算法，用于降低大语言模型的存储和推理开销，通过引入结构化高效的正交变换，实现了更快的迭代速度并增强了数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Kashin分解在权重量化中的应用存在计算效率低、数值不稳定等问题，特别是在大模型上难以扩展，且k-means初始化耗时瓶颈明显。

Method: 用带符号随机化的离散余弦变换（DCT）替换传统的稠密随机正交矩阵，将单次迭代复杂度由O(N^2)降低到O(N log N)；设计贪心交替更新算法，保障每个因子的2位聚类分布，并用解析初始化消除多次k-means的重复工作。同时复用主流优化模块（如OPTQ误差补偿、QuIP预处理）搭建高效JAX推理流水线。

Result: 新方法在OPT、Llama-2、Pythia等主流大模型上实现了4 bit/channel下与OPTQ、QuIP及其变种接近或优于的精度和推理速度。在Pythia-6.9B和Mistral-7B等极端高压测试下，Kashin-DCT展现出更好的数值稳定性，避免了QuIP系列出现的发散现象或计算NaN问题。

Conclusion: Kashin-DCT方法在保持高效、结构友好及硬件适应性的同时，大幅提升了大模型权数量化的稳定性与可扩展性，是现有方法的重要补充和改进。

Abstract: We revisit Kashin-decomposition-based weight quantization for large language models and propose an improved algorithm with stronger convergence properties and structured, efficient orthogonal transforms. The method retains the core factorization of each weight into two components -- one with bounded infinity norm and the other with bounded infinity norm after an orthogonal transformation -- but replaces the dense random orthogonal matrix with a sign-randomized Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), reducing the per-iteration cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$. The proposed greedy algorithm with alternating updates guarantees the four-peak distribution required for stable 2-bit clustering of each factor and admits closed-form initialization of cluster centers, removing the multi-restart k-means bottleneck of prior work. Composed with OPTQ-style sequential error compensation and QuIP-style incoherence preprocessing, the resulting JAX pipeline is competitive with OPTQ, QuIP, QuIP-RG and a fine-tuning- and vector-quantization-free variant of QuIP# at 4-bit per channel on OPT, Llama-2 and Pythia, with favorable wall-clock scaling. The bounded-$\ell_\infty$ factorization is also notably robust: on stress configurations where QuIP variants diverge to four-digit perplexity (Pythia-6.9B) or abort with NaNs in LDL back-substitution (Mistral-7B), Kashin-DCT remains numerically stable and stays close to FP16 baseline. At inference time, each weight decomposes into two 2-bit factor codes per channel that are structurally suited to native-2-bit hardware.

</details>


### [120] [Negative Self-Distillation: Learning to Reason by Avoiding Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11699)
*Rongcan Pei,Zhepei Wei,Shuyao Xu,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了负向自蒸馏（NSD）框架，以替代在大语言模型自提升中常用但存在副作用的正向自蒸馏（OPSD）方法，能更有效地提升模型在复杂推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD 会让学生模型过度模仿带有特权信息的答案，导致模型在复杂推理时无法表达不确定性，也压制了探索及自纠正行为，反而影响模型在复杂推理任务中的表现。

Method: 提出了 Negative Self-Distillation (NSD) 方法，用模型自身生成的“错误推理”作为负样本，通过引导学生模型远离这些错误推理分布来优化，而非直接模仿标准答案。同时，设计了动态 gating 机制，只对关键推理 token 进行惩罚，保护模型基础语言能力不受影响。

Result: 实验表明，NSD 在多个推理任务上持续优于 OPSD 及其他无标签自举强化学习（RL）方法。

Conclusion: NSD 能有效提升大模型复杂推理能力，兼顾模型行为提升与基础语言能力的保护，是比 OPSD 更优的自蒸馏新范式。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a popular paradigm for large language model (LLM) self-improvement, allowing models to act as their own teachers by leveraging privileged information such as ground-truth solutions. However, recent findings indicate that OPSD can severely degrade the performance of LLMs on complex reasoning tasks: By forcing the student to imitate an artificially confident reasoning trace conditioned on privileged information, OPSD inadvertently suppresses expressions of uncertainty and penalizes the exploratory, self-corrective behaviors required to solve challenging problems. To address this, we introduce Negative Self-Distillation (NSD), a new framework that optimizes LLMs by diverging from flawed reasoning rather than imitating privileged solutions. Instead of relying on ground-truth answers or external supervision, NSD uses the model itself to generate a question-specific negative condition (eg, acting as a ``careless reasoner'') and pushes the student's distribution away from this self-generated negative teacher. Naively applying unlearning objectives to achieve this divergence is problematic, as flawed reasoning tokens are confounded with basic linguistic tokens; indiscriminately penalizing both risks catastrophically degrading the model's foundational language capabilities. We resolve this by designing a dynamic gating mechanism that automatically identifies and isolates reasoning-critical tokens, ensuring gradient updates target only behavioral flaws while preserving the model's linguistic priors. Empirically, NSD consistently outperforms OPSD and other label-free, self-bootstrapping reinforcement learning (RL) baselines.

</details>


### [121] [The Eloquence submission for Task 2 of the Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11724)
*Jordi Luque,Lorenzo Concina,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了三种方法，针对21种语言的多语言多项选择问答任务，并均取得了比官方基线更优异的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着跨语言语音问答任务的兴起，高效、准确的多语言多模态方法需求增加。本文旨在提升大规模多语言多项选择问答的性能，推动该领域的发展。

Method: 1. 利用LoRA对Voxtral-Mini-3B进行微调，结合跨语种数据增强、ASR转录增强、带时间戳音频裁剪；2. 在冻结的Voxtral-24B上应用多模态上下文学习(ICL)纠正标签偏差；3. 无需训练的检索系统，采用三层声纹记忆（声学、语义、知识图谱）。

Result: 微调方法在第二阶段取得0.72宏准确率；ICL方法达到0.81，为最佳结果；训练自由检索系统取得0.68。三者均显著优于官方基线。

Conclusion: 所提三种方法均有效提升了多语言语音多项选择问答的表现，尤其多模态ICL方法效果最佳，为该领域任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper details the Eloquence team's approach to Task 2 of the 2nd MLC-SLM challenge at Interspeech 2026, which involves multilingual Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) across 21 languages. Three approaches are explored. First, we fine-tune Voxtral-Mini-3B via LoRA with cross-lingual data augmentation, ASR transcript augmentation and timestamp-aware audio cropping, achieving 0.72 macro-accuracy on evaluation Phase 2. Second, we apply multimodal in-context learning (ICL) to the frozen Voxtral-24B model to correct a strong label bias, reaching 0.81, our best result. Third, a training-free retrieval system based on a three-layer voice-anchored memory combining acoustic identity, semantic content, and a knowledge graph achieves 0.68. All three systems substantially outperform the official baseline.

</details>


### [122] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LOCUS方法，通过选择任务相关的低秩适应子空间，在不损失输出效用的前提下降低大语言模型生成序列长度和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的偏好对齐训练常常导致模型响应冗长，提升了推理成本但未带来实际效用提升，因此需要新的方法减少语言模型生成长度，降低成本，同时保证输出效用。

Method: 提出LOCUS方法：在冻结主干模型（backbone）的情况下，寻找到一个任务相关的低秩子空间，通过该子空间的后训练调整来同时优化生成序列长度和输出效用，只调整极少数参数。

Result: LOCUS在Anthropic HH-RLHF对话偏好任务上，对Pythia-2.8B可将续写长度减少39.84%，Qwen2.5-3B减少14.87%-17.58%，且仅需更新0.24%-0.28%的参数，同时内部效用评测基本无损。

Conclusion: LOCUS实现了显著的生成长度和推理成本降低，同时保持偏好对齐效用，为大模型部署带来更高效的服务能力。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [123] [RAG-Safety-Bench: Reliable Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11758)
*Adithiyan Rajan Indira Saravanan,Kathleen C. Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了RAG（检索增强生成）对大语言模型生成安全性回答的影响，并提出了新的评测基准RAG-Safety-Bench。结果表明RAG存在一定安全风险，基线安全措施不足以防止不当内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多终端用户希望将企业文档和知识库整合进LLM系统，确保RAG方案在安全性上的可靠性变得尤为重要。但当前RAG技术可能带来意想不到的安全副作用，需要深入理解其成因。

Method: 作者提出了RAG-Safety-Bench评测基准，从四种不同条件下评估RAG对生成安全性的影响：1. 非RAG；2. RAG使用包含有害请求答案的文档；3. RAG使用相关但未直接回答有害请求的文档；4. RAG使用随机安全文档。通过移除检索器质量干扰，清晰拆解RAG对安全性的影响。对五个开源LLM模型进行系统性实验分析。

Result: 实验证明，RAG方案下普通安全策略对有害内容的防护能力较弱，且良性与不安全能力呈负相关，即系统越“善良”安全越差，还发现即使是良性文档也可能被用于帮助生成不安全内容。

Conclusion: RAG提升了LLM检索合规信息的能力，但安全机制尚不完善，单纯依赖基线安全措施难以保障内容安全，未来应设计更强有力的安全防护并重视RAG安全评测。

Abstract: Allowing large language models (LLMs) to retrieve information from a set of trusted documents can increase reliability and reduce hallucination. However, recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can have unintended side effects on the overall safety of the generated responses, when prompted for harmful or dangerous content. A clearer understanding of the mechanisms leading to this result is needed, as increasing numbers of end users turn to RAG to incorporate corporate documents and knowledge bases into LLM-based systems. We introduce RAG-Safety-Bench, a benchmark to measure the safety impact of RAG on LLM models. By removing the confounding effect of retriever quality, and cleanly separating the problem into four conditions -- non-RAG, RAG with an oracle document containing the answer to the harmful request, RAG with documents related to the harmful request but without the specific answer, and RAG with random, safe documents -- the benchmark isolates the impacts of different factors in the observed safety degradation. We report results across five open-source LLMs, showing an inverse relationship between benign and unsafe capability, strong evidence that baseline safety guardrails do not lead to downstream safety guarantees in the RAG case, and model-specific support for previous findings that even benign documents can lead to unsafe generation in retrieval-enabled systems.

</details>


### [124] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习中分层差分隐私裁剪在语音大语言模型（speech-LLMs）中的应用，并提出了改进的α-split分配方法以提升模型性能和隐私防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习应用差分隐私时，常用的分层裁剪方法在面对结构差异极大的大型模型（如语音LLM，其声学编码与语言解码器的梯度规模差异极大）时会失效，导致训练崩溃或性能严重退化。因此，需要寻找针对这一问题的解决方法。

Method: 作者分析并实证了多种分层裁剪方法在不同speech-LLM架构上的失效原因，发现单一参数池的分配容易导致跨组件的预算塌缩。随后提出α-split方法，将编码器和LLM参数独立池化，独立分配裁剪预算，保证总体隐私保护不变并优化组件间的预算分配。

Result: α-split分配有效缓解了预算塌缩问题。实验显示，在适当选取的α值下，模型在维持整体（ε,δ）-DP的前提下，能显著提升WER表现，并在仅以2.6%的噪声开销的情况下，使编码器对基于说话人声音的梯度反演攻击的防护提升4.47倍。

Conclusion: 单池分层裁剪在存在大规模组件差异时不适用，提出的α-split双池方法能显著提升模型性能和隐私保护平衡，适用于speech-LLM这类异构结构的联邦学习场景。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [125] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文评估了大语言模型（LLM）在保持事实不变的情况下，是否能逆转新闻文本中的已知立场（framing），结果发现模型虽能识别文本立场，但难以成功逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多关注LLM生成、检测或评估改写文本的中立性，但鲜有实证探讨模型是否能在保留事实的前提下逆转文本立场。这对于理解模型在新闻编辑和舆论控制中的应用边界非常重要。

Method: 作者定义了三种文本立场表现：评价用词、归因实现、信息显著性。以60篇新闻文章为案例，针对三种干预强度，生成540对文本变体，确保事实保持一致，记录模型编辑内容，并在Qwen、DeepSeek与Kimi三个模型上进行实测。

Result: 实验表明，模型保持事实准确性的F1接近0.84，但文本立场逆转成功率极低（0.044-0.068），即便模型能正确识别文本立场及方向，逆转的成功率最高仅到0.071。

Conclusion: 作者指出，事实保持、立场识别和立场逆转三者之间存在明显鸿沟。能够识别立场并不意味着能有效逆转立场，对LLM自动编辑与校正新闻文本的能力提出了质疑。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [126] [The widening evaluation gap in medical large language model research 2023 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11770)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型的迭代速度远超医学临床证据的产生速度，绝大部分临床研究未能及时评估最新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型更新迅速，但临床证据积累缓慢，作者关注医学研究是否能跟上大模型的发展节奏。

Method: 分析2023年1月至2026年6月在14个临床领域的11628篇PubMed文献，统计采用的研究设计、模型评估时效、随机对照设计占比等。进一步通过对比分析“模型失效”与“研究时间差”等情况。

Result: 论文数量45倍增长，仅2.5%为随机、对照或前瞻性设计。研究发表时所评估模型的“时滞”从1.33季度延至6.08季度。新模型替代仅弥补了56%的时效性损失。超过六成随机试验评估的其实是停用模型。

Conclusion: 严谨(如随机试验)和前沿性存在冲突。这种冲突的根本在于模型选择滞后，而非单纯研究时间问题。

Abstract: Large language models are superseded every few quarters; clinical evidence takes years. We asked whether medical research is keeping pace with the systems it evaluates. PubMed returned 11,628 records for January 2023 to June 2026 across fourteen clinical domains, growing 45-fold; 2.5% used a randomised, controlled or prospective design. Evaluation lag, from a study's newest named model release to its own publication, widened from 1.33 to 6.08 quarters. Because discontinued models age mechanically, we benchmarked this against a counterfactual holding model composition fixed: migration to newer systems offset only 56% of the drift (95% CI 50-65). Randomised trials evaluated models a median 4.6 quarters older than other designs (P = 3 x 10^-19), yet among studies naming a model still under development no design differed from any other; 62% of randomised trials evaluated a discontinued family. Rigour and currency are in tension, and that tension reflects model selection rather than research timelines.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文对11种现代ASR和音频语言模型在英-约鲁巴语代码切换语音识别任务上进行了细致评测，发现当前主流模型在代码切换点和约鲁巴语token识别上表现普遍较差，即便整体WER较低也无法反映真实情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管当前ASR和音频语言模型在单语测试集上表现优异，但它们在多语种、特别是涉及广大非洲资源稀缺语种与代码切换语音的表现并不清楚。作者希望通过细化评测标准，深入理解现有系统在代码切换情况下的能力短板。

Method: 作者建立了一个包含2000条英-约鲁巴混合语音的确定性评测集，统一评测6种ASR模型和5种音频语言模型，并提出了切换点错词率、特定语言错词率、消除变音符号的WER等多种细致指标，弥补传统WER不足。

Result: 分析表明，整体WER无法反映模型代码切换处理能力。WER最优模型与最佳音频LM整体表现持平，但在切换点等细化指标上音频LM明显更优。同时大部分模型对约鲁巴语token基本无法识别，错误集中在切回约鲁巴语处。生成式音频LM还存在翻译、冗余、提示泄露等问题。

Conclusion: 单一WER不足以指导代码切换语音识别研究，需引入切换相关指标。当前主流模型对低资源、多音辨识和代码切换场景识别能力远未解决，亟需针对性改进。作者已开源相关评测资源，助力相关研究。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [128] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地评估了心血管筛查模型在去除潜在泄漏特征后的真实性能，结果显示模型间差异极小，并凸显了评价实践比模型本身更具决定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基于健康调查训练的心血管筛查模型报告了很高的准确率，但这些成绩是否由于特征泄漏（而非真正学习），以及新型表格基础模型是否改善了这一现象，仍需厘清。作者希望检验多模型在严格防止特征泄漏下的实际效能，并评估促进部署所需的各种模型性质。

Method: 作者基于2022年美国行为风险因素监测系统的44万数据，测试了10种分类器（包括线性、树集成、神经网络、可解释模型和表格基础模型），跨越5组风险递减的特征集。所有模型在区分度、校准性、公平性、推理代价等多维度进行了审计，并在2023年新队列上验证了泛化。

Result: 去除两个会泄漏诊断信息的特征后，所有模型AUC下降~0.05，且模型间AUC差异收敛在极小区间。可解释模型速度远超基础模型。模型存在性别与年龄公平性差异，但可通过后处理修复。模型在新队列迁移性好，AUC变化<0.002。

Conclusion: 文献中报道的高提升空间主要源自特征而非算法。提升透明度不仅无性能损失，还使公平与不确定性管理变得可审计。真实限制在于评价方式，而非模型容量。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [129] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索并提升了社交媒体中多语言混用文本（code-mixed text）下的字级语言识别能力，并发布了相关模型和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户频繁在一句话中切换多种语言（特别是在印度等多语言环境下），准确识别每个词的语言身份对于下游NLP任务极其重要。然而，现有的语言识别方法通常只适用于单语文本，无法对code-mixed文本做细粒度标注。

Method: 将token级语言识别任务建模为序列标注问题，基于Transformer的多语种预训练模型（MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa）进行微调，针对三种印度语言（印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语）进行了实验。还设计并公开了手工标注的评测基准，并提出两种利用平行语料生成混合文本的方法。

Result: 微调后的模型在三种语言的code-mixed数据上表现良好，展示了上下文嵌入对字级多语言识别任务的有效性。实验设置了高质量评测集合，效果显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 基于上下文Transformer结构的模型适用于社交媒体中的token级语言识别任务，推动了印度多语言NLP的发展，同时公开了数据集和模型以促进社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [130] [Epistemic orientation predicts legislative effectiveness among members of the US Congress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11865)
*Segun Aroyehun,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了美国国会议员使用证据导向型语言的差异，发现意识形态极端的议员较少使用证据导向型语言，而使用证据导向型语言更多的议员在立法效率上表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民主治理和集体决策需要以事实和证据为基础的沟通方式，以提升问责制。先前研究发现，自1970年代中期以来，美国国会证据导向型语言使用减少。本文旨在探究这种变化是否存在于不同个别议员之间，并分析其与立法行为和效率的关系。

Method: 研究以“证据与直觉差值（EMI）”为指标，量化国会议员在国会演讲及推特发文中的证据导向与直觉导向语言比例，并与议员的政治理念、制度角色、沟通情境及立法效率分数（LES）等数据联系分析。

Result: 研究发现，意识形态越极端的议员在国会演讲中使用证据导向型语言的比例越低。EMI在不同沟通平台（国会与推特）之间有一致性，但总体上推特上的EMI较低。此外，演讲中的EMI与议员的立法效率正相关，这种相关性在控制了各类变量后仍然显著。

Conclusion: 证据导向型语言不仅是国会整体话语的特征，也是影响个体议员沟通风格及立法效率的重要因素。提高个体议员的证据导向型表达可能有助于提升立法效果。

Abstract: Truth and evidence-based communication provide important foundations for democratic governance, accountability, and collective decision-making. Prior work shows that evidence-oriented language in US congressional floor speeches has declined since the mid-1970s, alongside broader changes in legislative productivity and polarization. This study shifts the analysis from congressional sessions to individual members of Congress to examine whether epistemic orientation varies systematically across legislators and whether it relates to political behavior and legislative effectiveness. Using the Evidence-Minus-Intuition (EMI) score, we measure the relative prevalence of evidence-oriented versus intuition-oriented language in congressional floor speeches and Twitter posts. We link these measures to legislator-level data on ideology, institutional position, communication context, and Legislative Effectiveness Score (LES). The results show that more ideologically extreme members use less evidence-oriented language on the congressional floor. EMI also exhibits cross-platform consistency with members who use more evidence-oriented language in floor speeches also being more evidence-oriented on Twitter, although EMI is lower on Twitter overall. Finally, EMI in congressional speeches is positively associated with individual legislative effectiveness, even after accounting for ideology and extensive political, institutional, demographic, topical, and communication volume controls. These findings suggest that evidence-oriented language is not only an aggregate feature of congressional discourse but also a meaningful attribute of individual-level legislative communication and effectiveness.

</details>


### [131] [Augustinian BabyLM: What Ostensive Definition Can and Cannot Teach a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11870)
*Lisa Bylinina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了将视觉信息用于语言模型词嵌入初始化的方法。通过将视觉特征作为种子，研究其对模型训练与表现的影响，发现视觉初始化能在特定任务中带来优势，但对主流语法任务影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型词嵌入训练依赖大语料，起始为随机初始化。本研究受圣奥古斯丁‘指示意义’理论启发，旨在探索用视觉信息作为词嵌入初始值，是否能影响语言模型的学习过程和下游任务表现。

Method: 在小型DeBERTa masked language model（训练语料1,000万词）中，将部分有视觉对应的词以视觉特征初始化嵌入，其余词仍为随机初始化。通过多项BabyLM基准与新设计的视觉属性任务，考察模型在语法与实体属性知识上的变化，并通过人为赋予新词视觉特征测试因果关系。

Result: 视觉初始化对相关词嵌入产生持续影响，对抽象语法任务几乎无效（表现与随机初始化无异），但在实体属性相关的任务（如COMPS）有显著提升；定制化视觉属性基准测试发现优势仅限被种子初始化的词，人工赋予视觉特征可转移优势。

Conclusion: 视觉嵌入初始化为语言模型提供了属性知识上的提升，且影响具有高度选择性。不过现有通用评测难以捕捉这类视觉优势，对相关评价方法提出了开放问题。

Abstract: A language model normally begins training with random word embeddings: whatever 'banana' means must be learned from training corpora. I implement St. Augustine's picture of word learning, meaning by ostension, for a small masked language model (DeBERTa) trained on 10M words: before training, visually grounded tokens receive embeddings derived from the image regions they label; other tokens start random. Visual initialization leaves a measurable imprint that lasts until the end of training. At the same time, the effect remains invisible under most BabyLM benchmarks, which probe abstract grammatical knowledge: visual initialization does not affect performance there. The only zero-shot exception is object-property knowledge (COMPS, Misra et al. 2023), where seeding helps in every configuration. To follow up on this result, I build a corpus-tailored version of the Visual-Property Swap benchmark (Lin et al., 2026), which tests color, material, size, and shape knowledge, with per-item training frequency and seeded status. Here, vision-seeded models have a persistent, seed- replicated advantage, confined to the seeded words. As a causal test, I show that synthetic grounding of previously unseeded words transfers the advantage to exactly those words. Function words and abstract vocabulary also receive strong visual seeds and retain them throughout training, and the training objective draws on them: held-out mask-prediction loss falls for these words in every seed. However, no benchmark I run registers this. What evaluation would pick this up remains an open question.

</details>


### [132] [Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11878)
*Varun Teja Chundru,Debasmita Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多信号流水线方法，结合DeBERTa-v3微调分类、MC Dropout不确定性量化和温度标定，实现了大型语言模型幻觉检测，在多个基准测试中取得了优异成绩，并验证了领域特定微调在转移学习中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的普及，其生成虚假或幻觉内容成为实际应用的严重隐患。现有检测方法存在准确性或泛化能力不足等问题，因此亟需更有效、通用的检测技术。

Method: 作者融合了三种信号：（1）对DeBERTa-v3进行微调用于分类；（2）通过Monte Carlo Dropout对模型预测不确定性进行量化；（3）温度标定提升模型输出的概率校准。该系统在HaluEval和SciFact等基准上进行了评估，并对不同任务类型和领域适应能力做了系统检验。

Result: 在HaluEval基准和常规任务中，该方法取得了F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977，具体F1分数为问答0.97，摘要0.96，对话0.82。MC Dropout进一步将准确率提升至93.2%。消融实验表明模型具备真实蕴含推理能力。用于优化生成器后将幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%。然而泛化到生物医学领域时性能下降，领域特定预训练可显著改善（F1=0.63, AUROC=0.81）。

Conclusion: 所提出的多信号检测方法在一般领域的幻觉检测上表现突出，但其跨领域迁移能力有限。领域相关的微调和预训练对于提升专用领域幻觉检测能力至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models generate fluent text that can contain unfaithful claims -- a phenomenon known as hallucination. We present a multi-signal detection pipeline combining fine-tuned DeBERTa-v3 classification, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty quantification, and temperature-scaled calibration for response-level hallucination detection. Evaluated on the HaluEval benchmark, our pipeline achieves F1=0.915 and AUROC=0.977 on general-domain tasks, with per-task F1 scores of 0.97 (QA), 0.96 (Summarization), and 0.82 (Dialogue). MC Dropout inference further improves accuracy to 93.2%. A context ablation study confirms the model performs genuine entailment reasoning rather than exploiting surface patterns, with summarization F1 dropping 24% when knowledge context is removed. Learning curve analysis reveals that 25% of training data captures 77% of full-data performance. Beyond detection, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to a Qwen2.5-0.5B generator, reducing its hallucination rate from 85.5% to 37.7% (55.9% relative reduction) as measured by our detector. Cross-domain evaluation on the SciFact biomedical benchmark shows that general-domain training transfers poorly (F1=0.52), motivating domain-specific fine-tuning. PubMedBERT fine-tuned on SciFact achieves F1=0.63 and AUROC=0.81, demonstrating that domain-matched pre-training is the strongest adaptation strategy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/varunteja99/hallucination-detection-nlp

</details>


### [133] [Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11892)
*Yingzhi Wang,Reem Alhazzani,Muhammad Alqurishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Nuha-Speech项目，构建了大规模阿拉伯语语音问答数据集，并基于Qwen-Omni模型训练、评测阿拉伯语语音大模型，旨在解决阿拉伯语在语音领域的资源稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语在多语种语音大语言模型中严重缺乏支持，缺少用于训练和评估的大规模数据集及完善基础设施，限制了阿拉伯语语音技术的发展。

Method: 1）构建了覆盖多种语音核心任务的150万训练样本阿拉伯语语音问答数据集；2）基于Qwen-Omni不同规模模型进行有监督微调；3）设计了包含多任务和专用指标的系统化评测框架。

Result: 构建了大规模阿拉伯语语音问答数据集，并基于该数据集成功训练和系统性评测了阿拉伯语语音大模型，建立了较为完整的阿拉伯语语音-LLM基础设施。

Conclusion: Nuha-Speech为阿拉伯语语音大模型的研究提供了稀缺资源和关键基础设施，有望推动阿拉伯语语音-LLM的发展和应用。

Abstract: As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

</details>


### [134] [Distance generalization in transformers: why bother with positional encoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11913)
*Daniel Henrik Nevermann,Claudius Gros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同位置编码方法和数据多样性对Transformer模型在距离泛化能力方面的影响。重点分析了模型在训练和推理时token间距离变化时的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在上下文长度变化下的泛化（out-of-distribution length generalization）已被广泛研究，但在固定上下文长度下，token间距变化（distance generalization）对于模型抽象能力的挑战尚未充分探讨。为此，作者希望明确现有位置编码、数据多样性等因素对距离泛化效果的影响。

Method: 设计了两种合成延迟复制任务：均需模型在源token和回忆token之间存在有限距离，并要求模型在不同的、训练中未见过的延迟下推理。对比三种位置编码（RoPE、ALiBi、NoPE），并分析训练集中的token间距多样性对性能影响，最后探讨距离迁移学习的表现。

Result: RoPE和ALiBi等位置编码能提升模型的距离泛化能力，但其机制和边界依然复杂。数据显示，增加训练数据中的距离多样性有助于泛化，但过度迁移可能带来负面效果。

Conclusion: 距离泛化能力的本质机制值得更深入研究，现有位置编码和数据多样性并非万能，理解Transformer在距离变化下行为规律对进一步提升模型泛化尤为关键。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution length generalization, namely to extrapolate a task from short to longer context, has been studied intensively for transformers. Here we focus on distance generalization, which probes performance when inter-token distances are changed between training and inference, while keeping a fixed context length. We construct two synthetic delay copy tasks, both involving finite distances between source and recall, where tokens are copied either fully or selectively, and test models on delays unseen during training. We address three questions: (A) Do positional encoding schemes such as RoPE and ALiBi improve distance resolution relative to no positional encoding (NoPE)? (B) How does data diversity, the number of inter-token distances seen in training, affect performance? (C) When is distance transfer learning positive or negative? We present a thorough investigation, finding that it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [HuRo: Robotizing Human Videos for Scalable VLA Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10706)
*Jinho Jeong,Se June Joo,Jaehyun Kang,Dongyun Kim,Yena Kim,Hanjung Kim,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将人类视频数据转化为机器人可用数据的流水线，并构建了大规模HuRo数据集，用于机器人视觉-语言-动作策略的预训练，显著提升了机器人任务成功率及泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集真实机器人交互数据成本极高且多样性受限，迫切需要低成本且高多样性的数据源。人类视频数据数量庞大、类型丰富，有望作为机器人学习的可行替代，但存在人-机体差异，需要将人类数据转换为机器人可用格式。

Method: 作者开发了一套机器人化处理流水线，将多源异构人类视频转化为机器人的视觉观测与动作轨迹，同时推断缺失的中间信号。利用该流水线构建了HuRo数据集，包含约63万机器人化过程、1.42亿帧图像。通过不同规模的数据对机器人进行视觉-语言-动作策略的预训练，并在四种真实操作任务上进行评测和消融实验。

Result: 随着机器人化预训练数据规模增加，任务整体完成率从51.5%提升至80.3%，空间和视觉分布外(OOD)任务完成率从34.9%提升至72.2%。消融实验表明，视觉机器人化强化了泛化鲁棒性，且端到端策略迁移效果优于仅转移视觉的方案。

Conclusion: 通过大规模机器人化处理的人类视频数据，可以高效地为机器人策略预训练提供监督信号，大幅提高机器人操作的成功率与泛化性能。本工作为人类视频赋能机器人学习提供了实用方案和新数据集。

Abstract: Human video datasets have emerged as a compelling alternative to expensive real-robot data, offering rich diversity at scale. To bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap, existing approaches either robotize videos in task-matched settings or address observation and action alignment separately at scale. In this work, we systematically examine whether robotized human videos can provide effective and scalable supervision for pretraining vision-language-action (VLA) policies. To this end, we develop a robotization pipeline that converts heterogeneous human videos into robot-aligned observations and action trajectories while inferring missing intermediate signals across annotation levels. Using this pipeline, we construct the HuRo dataset, comprising about 630K robotized episodes and 142M processed frames from five human-video sources. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, increasing robotized pretraining scale improves overall completion from 51.5% to 80.3% and OOD completion under spatial and visual shifts from 34.9% to 72.2%. Ablations further show that visual robotization improves OOD robustness and that end-to-end pretraining with retargeted actions outperforms visual-only transfer. Code and data are released on our website: https://3587jjh.github.io/HuRo.

</details>


### [136] [When Information is Worth the Risk: Behavioral Valuation for Hazardous Robotic Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10726)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Aamodh Suresh,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高风险环境机器人探索的信息-风险权衡新方法，通过调整路径选择的目标函数，在信息收益与风险规避之间实现灵活切换，并在大规模实验中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人在危险环境探索过程中，往往需要在收集有用信息和避免风险（如地形危险、辐射等）之间做权衡，如何构建和调整权衡标准成为提升机器人探索效率和安全性的关键。

Method: 文中提出了一种以Prelec概率加权为基础的风险-增强型行为信息目标（Risk-augmented Behavioral Information objective）。方法保持信念更新、传感器模型、物理风险模型及规划器不变，仅调整路径排名的目标函数参数，从而在探索策略中动态平衡信息量与风险量。

Result: 理论上分析了目标参数如何在高信息/高风险与低信息/低风险路径之间形成切换边界，并影响可行策略的帕累托前沿；大规模网格实验表明，仅靠目标函数的变化即可有效优化信息-风险权衡，风险敏感信息规划可显著减少机器人损失和感知中断。

Conclusion: 该方法在信息收集和风险规避间给出了直观、可解释的调节机制，具备与标准风险规划基线媲美的性能，并能够根据需求灵活实现保守或激进的探索。证明了机器人在危险探索环境下，不仅要关注不确定性减少的效用，更需要考虑为此付出的风险是否值得。

Abstract: Hazardous robotic exploration requires robots to map spatial risks, such as unsafe terrain, radiation, fire, mines, or structural damage, while operating where collecting information can itself cause failure. A highly informative path may expose the robot to hazards, terminate execution, and prevent future observations. Hazardous exploration therefore requires deciding not only where uncertainty is largest, but when reducing it is worth the risk. This paper introduces a valuation-layer view of this problem. We keep the belief update, sensor model, physical risk model, and finite-horizon informative planner fixed, and change only the scalar objective used to rank feasible paths. Within this framework, we introduce a risk-augmented Behavioral Information objective based on Prelec probability weighting, yielding an interpretable family of conservative-to-aggressive information-risk valuations. Theoretically, we show that valuation parameters create switching boundaries between high-information/high-risk and lower-information/lower-risk paths, and induce a transformed Pareto-frontier structure over feasible exploration policies. Large-scale failure-truncated grid-world experiments show that valuation alone reshapes the information-risk frontier. Shannon information planning remains a strong raw-information baseline, while risk-aware objectives can reduce hazard exposure and robot losses by avoiding failures that truncate future sensing. Risk-augmented Behavioral valuation is Pareto-competitive with standard risk-aware baselines and provides interpretable conservative and intermediate regimes. These results support a framework in which robots reason not only about how much uncertainty an action reduces, but whether that reduction is worth the risk required to obtain it.

</details>


### [137] [Lie-Algebraic Bell Recurrences for Arbitrary-Order Twist Jets and Parallel-Mechanism Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10748)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种任意阶运动学构造方法，可以在双螺旋框架下统一描述串联机构、并联机构以及刚性平台上的点场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前串联与并联机构的高阶运动学分析方法各自独立，存在理论统一和计算一致性上的难题。急需一种通用且严密的方法以系统化地计算机构的速度、加速度等高阶运动学量，并便于工程实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种任意阶运动学方法，将串联传播、并联机构约束和刚性平台点场通过双螺旋理论统一建模。该方法保留了圆柱副作为物理基元，用Bell多项式组织各阶导数，对并联机构采用Leibniz微分和三角递归，实现主动-被动变量的有序闭环。

Result: 通过对典型串联（非共面转轴的3C链）、并联（RR+RRR球腕）以及Hunt型6-RUS并联机构的算例验证，所有高阶导数的一致性误差优于$10^{-12}$（SI制），理论与独立数值验证完全吻合。

Conclusion: 该纯运动学方法可应用于任意阶串并联机构分析，理论统一、精度高、实施方便，适用于工程实际的主动-被动关节划分与高阶运动学分析。

Abstract: This paper develops an arbitrary-order kinematic construction that links serial propagation, parallel-mechanism closure, and rigid-platform point fields within one dual screw framework. A cylindrical joint is retained as one native physical block, with revolute and prismatic joints obtained as special cases. For each fixed joint axis, ordinary Bell polynomials organize the derivatives of the exponential factor; across a chain, the noncommuting factors remain in their physical order. Initial-frame prefix and terminal-resolved covariant formulas then produce equivalent representations of the serial twist jet. For a parallel mechanism, repeated Leibniz differentiation, with joint-level derivatives organized by Bell polynomials, yields an arbitrary-order triangular active-passive closure recurrence: the same passive Jacobian is solved at every derivative order at a regular configuration, while the right-hand side contains only prescribed active data and lower-order jets. The resulting platform twist jet is mapped exactly to the point-independent affine invariants of the velocity, acceleration, jerk, and snap fields. The validation is deliberately complementary: a generic 3C chain with noncoplanar axes and nonzero rotational and translational cylindrical coordinates tests ordered serial propagation, an RR+RRR spherical wrist tests active-passive closure, and a Hunt-type 6-RUS mechanism with six active revolute joints tests an independently reconstructed platform jet and its affine fields. Independent differentiation of the rigid motion, evaluation of the affine fields, and the differentiated branch closures all agree through fourth order with residuals below $10^{-12}$ in the corresponding SI units. The formulation is purely kinematic and applies at configurations where the selected active-passive partition is regular.

</details>


### [138] [Expressive Robotic Pianist: Mastering Complex Piano Repertoire with Graph-Mimic and Musical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10844)
*Yanhong Liang,Xianwei Liu,Chaojie Fu,Shaowen Cheng,Yanyan Yuan,Chengwei Zhuo,Xi Chen,Yongbin Jin,Wei Yang,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习与图优化相结合的机器人手演奏钢琴的控制框架，实现了机器人在多种钢琴曲目中的高保真与高表现力演奏，机器人的表现甚至难以被非专业听众与人类演奏区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然机器人动作精度提升很快，但在钢琴演奏中还难以模仿人类的流畅指法变换及动态情感控制，这大大限制了机器人的艺术表达能力。

Method: 采用强化学习控制体系，结合图优化方法生成自然的人类式预按压和按键指法，并通过物理声学模型调节按键力度，精准还原乐谱中的动态变化。

Result: 机器人在指法动作和动态表现力上均明显超越基线方法。在感知测试中，系统演奏更受欢迎，且对非专业听众几乎无法与真人演奏区分。各类曲风测试下也保持了高音准和表现力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了机器人钢琴演奏的表现力，使机器人不仅“弹对音”，还能“弹出感情”，向人类钢琴家的水平大幅迈进。

Abstract: Enabling robots to perform musical instruments with human-level expressivity represents a frontier in bridging the gap between mechanical precision and artistic interpretation. Despite advances in robotic dexterity, replicating the fluid finger transitions and nuanced dynamic control characteristic of human pianists remains a significant challenge. Through a reinforcement learning-based control framework, we demonstrate that a dexterous robotic hand can achieve high-fidelity performance across a diverse piano repertoire. Central to our approach is a graph-based optimization strategy that guides the robot to generate natural pre-press and key-press fingering strategies that closely resemble human movement patterns. To achieve expressive sound production, the control system is coupled with a physics-inspired acoustic model that modulates keypress velocity to accurately reproduce the dynamic variations specified in musical scores. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that our expressive control model significantly outperforms baseline methods in both finger morphology similarity and dynamic velocity accuracy. In a perceptual test involving participants from diverse listener groups, performances generated by our system are significantly preferred over baseline robotic performances and are indistinguishable from human performances for non-professional audiences. Furthermore, extensive experiments across multiple musical styles confirm that our method maintains high note-level accuracy while achieving expressive performance. Our approach provides a robust pathway for robotic systems to move beyond mere mechanical accuracy, elevating robotic musicianship to a level of expressive performance comparable to human pianists.

</details>


### [139] [ReactHuman: A Physics-Grounded Benchmark for Human-Like Reactive Decision-Making in Embodied Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10895)
*Yizhan Li,Jianxin You,Mengyang Xiong,Yinhuan Chen,Zicheng Zhao,Dekun Wu,Dongqing Zhang,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReactHuman基准，用于评估类似人类的多模态大语言模型(MLLM)在突发物理危险场景下的反应决策能力。结果显示当前MLLM在安全反应任务中表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MLLM在家庭机器人等实际应用的拓展，能否及时、合理地应对突发物理危险（如接住滑落的盘子或躲避掉落的刀具）成为其安全落地的关键。然而，现有评测多为被动物理理解或长时任务，缺乏对即时安全反应能力的考查。因此需要开发一种涵盖安全反应场景、注重物理合理性的全新评测方法。

Method: 作者提出了首个物理基础的“人类反应”基准（ReactHuman），利用模拟仿生人的环境，让MLLM在面临17类物理危险的场景（超1000个，含表观与物理属性反常的物体）中做出实时决策，并通过执行评估计划以得出实际后果。采用五项指标，从合理性、安全性和物理基础性三方面评分。

Result: 评测了七种代表性MLLM，发现其在应对物理突发危险时表现不佳，大约每三次应对有一次处理错误，存在凭固定倾向行动、信外观多于物理运动、错失关键拦截点等问题，而这些错误并未因模型规模的提升而减少。

Conclusion: ReactHuman不仅可细粒度诊断MLLM在安全反应方面的能力短板，还为后续开发物理基础、具安全感知的智能体提供了可扩展的训练信号，对MLLM实际落地应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Reacting to sudden physical hazards (catching a slipping plate, dodging a falling knife) is both a meaningful test of embodied intelligence and a hard requirement for deploying multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as the decision coreof household robots. Existing evaluations, however, probe intuitive physics passively through question answering over videos, or target deliberate, long-horizon tasks such as navigation and rearrangement; none measure whether a model can turn physical understanding into immediate, safety-critical action. We introduce ReactHuman, the first physics-grounded benchmark for human-like reactive decision-making, in which the evaluated MLLM acts as the brain of a simulated humanoid facing sudden household hazards; it spans 17 event families and over 1,000 bit-for-bit reproducible scenes with exact, annotation-free ground truth derived from 240 Hz rigid-body simulation, including adversarial objects whose appearance contradicts their physics (a foam anvil, a steel apple). We further design a five-metric suite that scores each reaction along three axes: reasonable, safe, and physically grounded. We physically execute every committed plan so that decisions have observable consequences. With this harness we evaluate seven representative MLLMs. Results show that reactive safety is far from solved: models mishandle roughly one hazard in three, act from fixed dispositions rather than the observed scene, trust appearance over motion, and miss interception points at meter scale even when the chosen action is correct; none of these failures shrink with model scale. ReactHuman thus offers both a fine-grained diagnosis and a scalable training signal toward physically grounded, safety-aware embodied agents. The benchmark can be found here: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Alan123/reacthuman-benchmark-scaled

</details>


### [140] [Planning along Differentiable Charts of Constraint Manifolds with General-Purpose IK Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10905)
*Thomas Cohn,Seiji Shaw,Harel Biggie,Travis Manderson,Nicholas Roy,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新方法，通过利用反函数定理，从常规的前向运动学雅可比矩阵中计算解析逆运动学（IK）参数化的梯度，实现了机器人在存在运动学等式约束下的可行轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在存在运动学等式约束时，其可行运动受限于配置空间的零测子流形，传统方法难以高效进行轨迹优化。现有大多数IK函数非易于微分，阻碍了基于梯度的优化，因此亟需一种既能保持解析IK优势、又易于进行梯度计算的新方法。

Method: 作者利用反函数定理，通过常规的前向运动学雅可比矩阵直接恢复所需的解析IK梯度。此外，提出了最小二乘的定义域扩展及优化友好的可达性约束描述，保证在不可达区域依旧有有效的梯度信号。

Result: 作者通过数值实验和下游任务（如RB-Y1机械臂抓取并放置箱子的硬件演示）验证了方法的有效性，说明该方法在实际应用中具有良好表现。

Conclusion: 本文新方法为在运动学等式约束下的机器人轨迹优化带来了高效可微的IK梯度计算方式，有望提升相关机器人的性能和优化效率。

Abstract: Planning trajectories for robot manipulators under kinematic equality constraints restricts feasible motions to a measure-zero submanifold of the configuration space, requiring special algorithmic treatment. A promising strategy is parametrizing the set of feasible configurations using analytic inverse kinematics (IK). Bespoke analytic IK functions can be written to be differentiable, a necessary property for gradient-based trajectory optimization. But the vast majority of IK functions are computed by automated meta-solvers like IKFast, and are difficult to modify for differentiability. We present a new approach for computing gradients of analytic IK parameterizations: we leverage the inverse function theorem to recover the desired gradients from the ordinary forward kinematic Jacobian. Furthermore, we present a least-squares domain extension and an optimization-amenable description of the reachability constraint, which preserves gradient signal outside the reachable workspace. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through numerical experiments and downstream tasks, including a hardware demonstration of an RB-Y1 picking up a box and placing it on a table. Project website: https://cohnt.github.io/inverse-function-theorem-parameterization/

</details>


### [141] [IMLE-VLA: Fast Single-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10915)
*Kian Hosseinkhani,Qinhe Peng,George Shramko,Mehran Aghabozorgi,Jianing Qian,Tristan Engst,Alireza Moazeni,Dinesh Jayaraman,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMLE-VLA是一种改进的视觉-语言-动作策略方法，通过用单步生成器替换原有的多步采样动作头，大幅提升推理速度和动作流畅性，同时保持强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略虽然有强的泛化能力，但依赖于连续动作头的多步采样，导致推理效率低、机器人动作卡顿，影响真实场景下的应用。

Method: 提出用cIMLE（条件隐式最大似然估计）训练的单步条件生成器，取代原本需多次采样的动作头，实现一步生成动作。cIMLE目标能覆盖多模态动作，避免模式坍塌，同时免除反复采样。

Result: 在LIBERO基准40任务上，IMLE-VLA推理频率提升3.67倍，动作吞吐量最多提升11倍，成功率高达98%，在频率与泛化性上均优于全部基线；真实机器人测试中，实现更平滑、更快的动作，推理时间减少近4-7倍。

Conclusion: IMLE-VLA无需多步采样，极大提升推理效率和实际表现，同时保持甚至提升泛化与稳健性，适用于真实机器人操作，有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language backbones to achieve strong cross-task generalization. A leading design couples this backbone with a dedicated continuous action head trained via diffusion or flow matching. However, such heads rely on iterative multi-step sampling, for example 10 Euler steps in $π_{0.5}$. This creates an inference bottleneck that produces stop-and-go movement in the robot and slower task completion. We introduce IMLE-VLA, which replaces the iterative action head with a single-step conditional generator trained via conditional Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (cIMLE). The cIMLE objective promotes multimodal action coverage, avoiding the mode collapse of naive regression heads while eliminating multi-step sampling entirely. When IMLE-VLA is applied to $π_{0.5}$, it increases inference frequency 3.67x (55 Hz vs. 15 Hz), enabling up to 11x higher action throughput. On the 40-task LIBERO benchmark, IMLE-VLA achieves the highest average success rate (98.0%) among all baselines while leading in inference frequency. Under the test-time perturbations of LIBERO-plus, IMLE-VLA retains $π_{0.5}$'s robustness while other baselines degrade sharply, confirming that the cIMLE head preserves generalization. Real-world experiments on a Franka Emika Panda across four tasks demonstrate smoother motion (2.2x to 3.0x lower jerk) and faster task completion, with IMLE-VLA outperforming $π_{0.5}$ on every task and reducing average VLA inference time per episode by 3.9x to 6.6x. Videos and code are available at https://kianhk6.github.io/IMLE-VLA/

</details>


### [142] [ObstaDiff: Generalizable Diffusion Policy Learning via Obstacle-aware Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10918)
*Jiawen Wang,Kevin Yao,Khalid Jawed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ObstaDiff，一种结合扩散策略和轻量级视觉编码器的模仿学习框架，在复杂障碍物环境下实现更好的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有模仿学习方法仅适用于环境干净、无障碍物的场景，缺乏对实际复杂环境、障碍物感知和避让的能力，限制了其在真实世界应用中的泛化能力。

Method: 提出ObstaDiff框架：引入带有障碍感知的视觉编码器，将目标－障碍－背景结构化表达，并设计下游的轨迹生成策略，使末端执行器考虑障碍环境地向目标移动。

Result: ObstaDiff在温室真实机器人任务中（共366次实验），实现了75.41%的平均任务成功率和8.20%的平均障碍碰撞率，效果优于主流模仿学习基线方法。

Conclusion: ObstaDiff有效提升了机器人在障碍密集、杂乱环境下的泛化能力和操作安全性，对农业等复杂应用具有现实价值。

Abstract: Imitation learning has achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation, yet most existing approaches assume clean backgrounds and lack explicit mechanisms for obstacle-aware motion generation. Extending such policies to cluttered, real-world scenes with unstructured obstacles remains a key generalization challenge. We present ObstaDiff, a decomposed diffusion-policy framework with a lightweight obstacle-aware visual encoder. ObstaDiff extracts a structured target-obstacle-background representation, enabling the downstream alignment policy to generate end-effector trajectories toward a target-centered bottleneck pose while reasoning about surrounding obstacles. We evaluate ObstaDiff on 61 real-robot greenhouse trials per method (366 executions in total). ObstaDiff achieves 75.41% average task success and 8.20% average obstacle collision rate, outperforming representative imitation-learning baselines and improving generalization in cluttered agricultural scenes.

</details>


### [143] [Testing Between the Test Cases: Proving End-to-End Steering in Conditions You Never Drove](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10951)
*Menuka Ghalan,Charles Rodgers,Zachary D. Asher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将形式化验证方法应用于自动驾驶车辆的AI模型评估，以解决仿真测试难以全面覆盖真实场景的问题。通过对多天气条件下训练的神经网络模型，利用形式化方法预先计算车辆偏离车道的风险，无需重复模拟驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶AI模型即使通过了所有已知测试场景，依然可能在现实未知条件下失败。需要一种方法能系统性且全面地评估模型在各种干扰下的安全性，而不仅依赖有限的仿真测试。

Method: 作者在CARLA仿真环境下，分别在简单（晴天）和复杂（多种天气）条件下训练了方向盘控制的端到端神经网络模型。然后，使用bound propagation（一种读取神经网络权重的形式化验证方法）估算模型在各种扰动强度下偏离车道的程度，无需重复驾驶仿真。该方法可在极短时间覆盖大量场景组合。

Result: 形式化验证发现了仅在晴天训练的模型在某些条件下会失效，这些失效点是仿真测试未能覆盖到的。此外，该方法还能初步识别测试用例之间潜在的失效区域。

Conclusion: 形式化验证可以作为仿真测试的重要补充，有望成为自动驾驶系统验证与确认流程中的一部分，从而提升模型的安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: AI-based automated vehicle testing is challenging because a model that passes every test condition can still fail in the real world. Formal verification offers a way to directly address this gap. On a simulated highway and an arterial road we trained two small end-to-end steering networks each in CARLA, one on clear conditions alone and one on clear, fog, night and low sun. All four models were driven against a 2.19 ft lane-departure budget. Without driving again, we used bound propagation, a formal method that reads the trained weights, to compute how far steering can drift at every disturbance strength between two captured images. One calculation covers more than a campaign could drive: on the arterial it spans 133 poses, where ten intensities each would be 10^133 combinations, in minutes on one GPU. Not only did formal verification find conditions that broke the clear-trained policy without simulation testing, it provided some preliminary evidence for potential failures between the test cases. Our overall conclusion is that formal verification is a viable complement to simulation, and could be adopted as a part of verification and validation for automated driving.

</details>


### [144] [LTLDiff: Finite Linear Temporal Logic-Guided Data Generation and Diffusion Policies for Multi-agent Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11043)
*Chuhan Meng,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出LTLDiff框架，通过结合有限线性时序逻辑（LTLf）与扩散策略，提升多智能体机器人协作操控任务中的协调性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体扩散策略在需要精确时序或同时操作的任务中，经常出现同步失效和动作顺序错误，导致任务失败。为提升协调性和成功率，需要引入更具规律约束的方法。

Method: LTLDiff结合了LTLf规范学习和扩散策略。具体为：1）通过大规模语言模型从自然语言指令中学习任务相关的LTLf公式；2）用抽象语法树将逻辑公式转换为定长向量嵌入，用于逻辑引导的数据收集与扩散策略训练，促使轨迹符合任务顺序和协调要求。

Result: 在多智能体操控任务中，与传统方法相比，LTLDiff框架显著提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: LTLDiff能有效指导多智能体实现时序一致、协作协调的操作，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent robotic manipulation tasks require coordination among agents to satisfy task-level temporal, logical, and safety constraints. Recently, diffusion policies have been used to perform the task. However, they still suffer from desynchronization, incorrect action ordering, and coordination failures in tasks that require simultaneous or sequential multi-agent interaction. Therefore, LTLDiff is proposed as a framework that combines Finite Linear Temporal Logic (LTLf) specification learning for both the generation of demonstrations and learning via diffusion policies. Each task has a specific LTLf formula that is learned from a set of natural language instructions using a large-scale language model. To enable a fixed-dimensional vector embedding of the learned specification from the language model, LTLf uses an abstract syntax tree representation scheme. This embedding of logic serves as a condition for (i) logic-guided data collection and (ii) diffusion-based policy training, encouraging trajectories that are consistent with the desired ordering and coordination requirements. Experiments on multi-agent LTLDiff manipulation tasks demonstrate improved task success rates compared to the baseline. Together, these contributions demonstrate the effectiveness of LTLDiff for coordinated multi-agent manipulation.

</details>


### [145] [Gait-Dependent Effects on Quadruped Locomotion for Load-Carrying using Passive Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11059)
*Giovanni B. Dessy,Claudio Semini,Victor Barasuol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了被动机械接口（如被动机械臂）的刚度和阻尼选择如何在不同步态和载荷条件下影响四足机器人负载行走的表现。通过仿真比较了不同被动臂阻抗配置，并用ZMP边际（稳定性指标）进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相比于主动操作机构，被动机械接口能显著减轻四足机器人负载搬运时的自重负担，但其机械阻抗会直接影响负载与步态之间的动态耦合。论文旨在揭示被动臂阻抗参数的取值对步态稳定性与负载行走性能的影响规律，为参数选择提供理论依据。

Method: 采用仿真分析方法，将被动臂设置为不同的阻尼-刚度组合（包括过阻尼和欠阻尼），并在平地上进行爬行步态和小跑步态下的负载搬运实验。通过测量被动关节振荡和用ZMP边际评估稳定性，系统对比不同配置的表现。

Result: 仿真结果发现，在爬行步态下，欠阻尼阻抗配置会大幅增加被动关节振荡，导致支撑多边形内的ZMP边际降低，降低了机器人的稳定性。小跑步态主要用于测试被动臂的动态响应，而不是直接用于ZMP比较。

Conclusion: 文中通过步态-负载-刚度-阻尼的多参数映射，总结了被动机械接口参数对负载行走稳定性的影响规律，为实际参数设计提供了定量参考。

Abstract: Passive mechanical interfaces offer a lightweight alternative to actuated manipulators for quadruped payload carrying, but their impedance directly couples the payload dynamics with the locomotion pattern. This paper analyzes how passive-arm stiffness-damping selection affects payload-carrying locomotion under different gait and payload conditions. We compare damped and underdamped passive-arm impedance configurations in simulation during flat-ground locomotion. For crawl gaits, where the support polygon remains well defined, the results show that underdamped impedance increases passive-joint oscillations and can reduce the ZMP margin with respect to the support polygon. Trot is retained as a dynamic excitation case for the passive arm, but it is not used for direct ZMP-margin stability comparison. The results are summarized in gait-payload-stiffness-damping maps, where ZMP-margin reduction is evaluated for crawl gaits and trot is retained only as a passive-arm excitation case.

</details>


### [146] [Freehand Sketching for End-User Programming of Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11078)
*Riwa Karam,Ian Kuo,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种通过手绘草图来控制机器人群体形成几何图形的方法，对非专业用户友好，实用性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人群体应用中，非专业用户难以直接、直观地制定复杂空间队形，因此需要一种无需编程基础、低门槛的人机交互方式。

Method: 设计了一个“草图到机器人群体”界面，允许用户自由绘图，系统自动从草图中提取目标形成点、生成刚性图、分配机器人、并分布式控制群体实现目标形状，同时保证不会出现镜像形队列。通过20名参与者、42个图形的用户实验来评估界面的可用性。

Result: 界面获得平均84.25的SUS可用性得分，表明用户普遍认为该界面易用且直观。实验参与者顺利用手绘方式完成了多种几何图形的群体控制。

Conclusion: 自由手绘为非专业用户与机器人群体协作提供了直观高效的交互抽象，无需用户具备机器人或编程知识。

Abstract: Robot swarms are increasingly used in applications where accessible interaction with non-expert users is desirable. This paper investigates freehand sketching as an end-user programming interface for specifying robot swarm geometries. Users communicate spatial intent through a drawing, while the swarm autonomously extracts target formation points, constructs a rigid formation graph, assigns robots to formation nodes, and executes distributed formation control with a guarantee against unintended reflected formations. The resulting sketch-to-swarm framework is evaluated through a human study examining the usability of freehand formation specification. Twenty participants generated $42$ geometric shapes, and the interface achieved a mean System Usability Scale score of $84.25$, which conventionally indicates high perceived usability. The results support freehand sketching as an intuitive interaction abstraction for human-swarm collaboration without requiring robotics or programming expertise.

</details>


### [147] [RIDE: Relocalization-Informed Depth Estimation with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11079)
*Jiarong Lian,Zhe Xiao,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Ruizhi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIDE方法结合了基于PnP-RANSAC的稀疏度量深度观测和预训练视频深度模型的几何先验，实现了机器人RGB视频流的高精度密集深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Render-match-PnP定位方法虽能进行相机位姿恢复，但其在密集深度估计中的潜力常被忽视。本文旨在充分利用其中蕴含的几何信息，提升机器人感知的深度估计能力。

Method: 提出RIDE框架：输入为机器人RGB视频流和具有度量尺度的3D Gaussian Splatting模型，将PnP-RANSAC找到的稀疏对应点生成的度量深度与预训练视频深度模型的几何先验融合。同时，整合全局和局部深度校正，以及时间记忆机制，以应对观测间歇和初始化之后的短时观测空档。

Result: 在公开RGB-D视频上训练后，无需特定微调即可在机器人真实序列上测试。实验对比了仅用尺度校准的方法，RIDE在深度估计精度和时序一致性上都有显著提升。

Conclusion: RIDE证明了将定位几何与预训练深度模型结合，可同时增强位姿恢复和密集机器人感知能力，为下一代机器人感知技术提供了新思路。

Abstract: Render--match--PnP relocalization establishes correspondences between query image pixels and 3D map points for camera pose recovery, but their potential to support dense depth estimation is often overlooked. To exploit this geometric information, we present RIDE, which estimates dense metric depth from a robot's RGB stream. Given a metrically scaled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, RIDE combines sparse metric depth observations derived from PnP-RANSAC inlier correspondences with the geometric prior of a pretrained video-depth model. To handle uneven and intermittent observations, it integrates global and local depth correction with temporal memory, supporting depth estimation through short observation gaps after metric scale initialization. Trained on public RGB-D videos, RIDE is evaluated on robot sequences without fine tuning. Experiments show improved depth accuracy and temporal consistency over scale-only calibration, demonstrating how localization geometry can support both pose recovery and dense robot perception.

</details>


### [148] [Harness Robotic OS: A Unified Embodied-Agent Runtime for Closed-Loop Quadruped Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11225)
*Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Haoxiang Jie,Gang Liu,Hongjie Yan,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自主物业巡检的统一机器人操作系统（HROS），实现了多模态感知、自主技能、认知推理、人机交互与企业响应的高效整合，并在实景中验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四足机器人巡检系统在集成感知和自主能力时，多依赖于任务特定接口，导致上下文协调、知识复用和系统可控演化难以实现，无法满足实用化部署需求。

Method: 提出HROS统一运行时架构，分为机器人运行体（robot runtime）、具身自主技能、认知体运行时（cognitive agent runtime）、交互与运营层（interaction and operations planes），以共享上下文串联实体状态与智能推理，融合语音任务交互、层级记忆存储以及安全自进化闭环。通过Argos原型系统将上述方案集成至四足机器人平台，并结合多种导航、感知及分析子模块进行实际验证。

Result: 在住宅小区环境中，系统实现了100%的路径点到达率，户外定位精度优于10cm，本地障碍响应延迟低于200ms，典型安全隐患检测准确率为85-95%，报警与结构化报告的生成率达99%。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了HROS架构下巡检闭环导航与检测的可靠性，该平台为具备记忆增强、语音交互和可持续进化能力的巡检机器人提供了可扩展的软件基础。

Abstract: Autonomous property inspection requires more than robust robot navigation: a deployable system must connect heterogeneous sensing, reusable autonomy capabilities, multimodal scene understanding, human interaction, and enterprise response within a traceable operational loop. Existing quadruped inspection systems commonly integrate these functions through task-specific interfaces, making contextual coordination, knowledge reuse, and controlled adaptation difficult. This paper presents \textit{Harness Robotic OS} (HROS), a unified embodied-agent runtime, and Argos, its realization for residential-community inspection. HROS organizes the system into robot runtime, embodied autonomy skills, cognitive agent runtime, and interaction and operations planes. A shared context connects physical state with agent reasoning; streaming ASR/TTS supports voice-based mission interaction; hierarchical working, episodic, and semantic memory preserves operational knowledge; and a safety-gated self-evolution loop converts execution traces into versioned candidate updates without permitting unconstrained online modification. The Argos prototype integrates a Vbot quadruped, Fast-LIO2 localization and mapping, Hobot-Stereo depth perception, PCT-Planner global planning, EGO-Planner local motion generation, and OpenClaw-orchestrated Qwen3-VL inspection analysis. Experiments in a residential property environment achieved 100\% waypoint reachability, outdoor localization error below 10~cm, local obstacle-response latency below 200~ms, representative hazard-detection rates of 85--95\%, and 99\% success in alarm delivery and structured-report generation. These results validate the deployed navigation and inspection closed loop, while HROS provides an extensible software foundation for memory-augmented, voice-aware, and continuously improvable embodied inspection agents.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Noise Steering: Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning for Generative Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11270)
*Pengfei Zhang,Teng Sun,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种创新的Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning（DLSRL）框架，实现了预训练生成机器人策略的更高效调控和适应，提升了机器人操作任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法只能对噪声空间进行引导，难以在生成过程中调节中间动作表征，导致机器人策略性能低效、容易退化，需要新方法提升生成策略的调控能力和适应性。

Method: 提出DLSRL框架，设计了一个actor网络，预测两个不同的潜变量：一是引导行为生成的初始噪声潜变量，二是用于中间特征调制的动作表征潜变量。动作表征潜变量被映射成adapter特征，并通过残差连接注入到动作生成过程的中间层，实现动作表征的直接调整。整个生成器保持冻结，无需更新基础策略。

Result: 在多种生成策略架构和机器人操作任务上实验，结果表明DLSRL能够显著加速机器人策略的在线自适应过程，同时达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DLSRL框架不仅提高了策略适应效率，还在无需更新底层策略的情况下，实现了机器人生成策略的表达性和性能提升，适用于多种生成策略和机器人任务。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies learn expressive action priors from demonstrations. However, existing reinforcement learning methods only steer the noisy space but fail to modulate intermediate action representations during the generation process, resulting in performance degradation and inefficiency. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning (DLSRL) framework, which complements initial-noise steering with representation-level control inside the frozen generator. Specifically, our actor network predicts two distinct latent variables: an initial-noise latent variable that steers behavior generation, and an action-representation latent variable for intermediate feature modulation. Moreover, this representation latent variable is mapped to adapter features and ingeniously injected into the hidden states of intermediate action tokens via residual connections. Our dual-control design enables direct adjustment of action representations without updating the base policy. Experiments across generative policy architectures and robotic manipulation tasks show that DLSRL effectively accelerates online robot policy adaptation and achieves competitive performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}.

</details>


### [150] [2AM: Grounding Agent-Side Memory as Guidance for Steerable Action Models in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11308)
*Yutong Hu,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，探究长时序机器人操作任务中，任务记忆是否一定要存在于动作策略内，通过限制工具范围但扩展接口带宽，对多模态智能体和动作模型进行分工，以提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人智能体系统在解决长时序操作任务时，通常混合视觉-语言智能体（VLA）、规划器和几何工具，导致提升的归因不清楚——究竟是系统观察能力强了，还是动作工具改进了？为澄清系统性能的真正来源，有必要将智能体的任务记忆与动作模型的执行能力明确分开。

Method: 设计一个名为2AM的系统，将智能体设为唯一的任务记忆载体，由单一的基于RGB的动作模型实现动作执行。智能体整理交互历史为子任务语言提示和可选的2D操作提示。通过为演示数据添加结构化提示标签，并采用条件丢弃、空间噪声和时序扰动训练，提高模型对不完美提示的容忍度。

Result: 在不借助深度信息、在线几何推理或基于规划的物体运动的情况下，2AM在LIBERO-Mem任务中的平均完成率达到76.3%，远高于当前最佳基线（14.8%），宽松与严格成功率分别为63.0%和11.8%。

Conclusion: 长时序任务的任务记忆可完全保留在智能体侧，动作模型能力不仅取决于训练的策略，还强烈依赖于智能体给出的提示精度。这一结果简化了系统设计，并有助于分离任务失败或成功的归因。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires memory, but not necessarily inside the action policy. To address such tasks, current agentic systems often combine VLAs with planners and geometric tools, sometimes using additional depth or calibrated geometry. These systems confound attribution: gains may come from richer observations or alternative motor tools, while failures may stem from either the policy or an under-specified language interface. We isolate this question through a deliberately constrained design: less tool breadth, but greater interface bandwidth. 2AM makes a multimodal Agent the sole holder of task memory and a single RGB-based, episodically stateless Action Model the sole executor of task-relevant motion. The Agent compiles interaction history into subtask language and optional 2D grasp, place, and move hints that bind its physical intention at different time scales. To teach this steerability to the VLA, we augment demonstrations with structured hint labels and train under condition dropout, spatial noise, and temporal jitter to tolerate imperfect Agent outputs. On LIBERO-Mem, without depth, online geometry, or planner-based object motion, 2AM reaches 76.3% average completion, a 61.5-point improvement over the strongest reported baseline of 14.8%, together with 63.0% relaxed and 11.8% strict success. These results show that task memory can remain Agent-side. They further show that Action Model capability depends not only on what the policy has learned, but on how precisely the Agent can steer it.

</details>


### [151] [Modular Kinematic Reduction of Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly and Defect Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11338)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种路径组装闭合差分映射（PACDM）框架，用于模块化封闭链机器人的解析高效建模和求解，并显著提升了求解速度和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭链机构由于存在非线性闭合约束，主动与被动坐标耦合，导致其模块化运动学建模与分析极为复杂，亟需高效且通用的建模与解算方法。

Method: 建立了PACDM框架：以局部变换路径的对比与错配，通过SE(3)对数和相关雅可比矩阵构建闭合约束；利用缺陷同伦法从初步近似逐步恢复满足约束的被动变量；利用隐式微分得到主动-被动的微分映射，并在运动规划中结合预测-修正续算法提升效率。

Result: 在一个包含两路径和三路径闭链模块的七自由度重载机械臂实例上，实验表明PACDM与Simscape Multibody对比下，轨迹均方根误差小于8.5×10^-10弧度，且预测-修正法的计算速度比逐点同伦提升约45.8倍。

Conclusion: PACDM框架显著提升了闭链机构的模块化建模与高效闭合解算能力，对复杂机器人结构的分析与控制均具重要意义。

Abstract: Closed kinematic chains complicate modular modeling by coupling active and passive coordinates through nonlinear closure constraints. This paper presents a Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping (PACDM) framework for modular closure resolution and kinematic reduction. Each closure element compares two ordered transformation paths with common endpoints, with their mismatch expressed through the logarithm on SE(3) and the corresponding Jacobian assembled from local transformation derivatives. Multi-path modules are constructed from a minimal set of pairwise closure elements, while rank-revealing analysis selects locally independent scalar constraints. A defect homotopy recovers closure-consistent passive coordinates from approximate estimates along a feasible and regular continuation path. At regular configurations, implicit differentiation yields the local active-to-passive differential mapping, which is subsequently used in a predictor-corrector continuation procedure for prescribed motion. The framework is evaluated on a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty manipulator containing two-path and three-path closed-chain modules. Comparison with Simscape Multibody yields trajectory root-mean-square errors below 8.5 x 10^-10 rad, while predictor-corrector continuation is approximately 45.8 times faster than applying defect homotopy at every trajectory sample.

</details>


### [152] [Morphology-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Wheeled-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11357)
*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一套可以将人类动作重定向为轮式仿人机器人（Galaxea R1 Pro）可执行动作的新管线，支持运动和操作的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于运动重定向的大多数研究集中于有腿机器人，对于轮式仿人平台的身体动作与操作结合研究较少，尤其是如何将人的下肢动作合理映射到没有腿关节的轮式基座并且兼顾手部操作能力。

Method: 基于GMR的广义运动重定向与BeyondMimic的R1 Pro仿真学习框架，提出了一条端到端管线，从多数据集的人体SMPLX动作转成Galaxea R1 Pro 机器人可执行的本体运动+手臂操作串联。具体步骤包括：身体预处理、基座归一化、基于形态的微分逆解算、基于肩的分层手臂重定向、躯干持续替代实现弯腰和下蹲，并结合参考扭转-驱动的轮式运动规划。同时在Isaac仿真实验室训练了21维的BaseDecode策略，采用定向关节限幅、重点上半身跟踪和分阶段轮接触奖励。

Result: 实现了一整套从人体动作数据到可物理执行的轮式仿人机器人动作的桥梁，不再仅是可视化的动作重定向。定量策略对比将在后续版本发布。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了一条面向物理执行的，支持耦合运动-操作的轮式仿人机器人动作重定向新范式，推进了从人体多关节动作到异构机器人平台实用动作再现的研究。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [153] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoTrussRover系统，将可变几何桁架(VGT)、轮式底盘与接触语义的规划和控制结合，实现高维超冗余结构的低维高效运动规划。系统通过物理约束投影，针对不同障碍适应动作，极大减少了规划计算量，并在实物原型上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前重构型机器人在面对复杂障碍时，形态变化与机动底盘的协调成为高维、难以高效解决的问题，尤其是负载结构的高维任务协调缺乏有效的低维表示。本文试图通过提炼机械协调的接触语义原语、引入物理约束适应方法，提升运动规划效率。

Method: 系统结合电驱动VGT与轮式底盘，通过运动学分析提取4种接触语义原语，将21成员的高维协调归纳为低维描述。提出物理约束投影技术，在遇到不同障碍时，仅对物理约束不满足的阶段局部重规划，并用全空间QP优化轨迹，保证动作跟踪与误差修正。

Result: 相比全部重规划，方法在跨越高度0.10m到0.075m阶段减少了63.7%的目标函数计算量。接触阶段可行性分析覆盖0.10-0.46m的台阶高度（1.08至4.97轮半径），贴近理论极限。实物原型可通过2.11轮半径的障碍，验证了该低维表示及适应机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为负载型重构机器人提供了高效、可迁移的运动规划框架，通过接触语义提取与局部重规划显著提升了运动适应性和计算效率。在应对多样障碍环境时，该方法有望成为高维机器人系统运动规划的有力工具。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control. We solve one source traversal and extract four contact-semantic primitives that describe coordination among 21 members. Physics-constrained projection adapts them to unseen step heights with the same contact topology. When every phase remains feasible, adaptation does not recompute the complete motion. If one phase violates the new physical constraints, only that phase is recomputed. A full-space QP then tracks the adapted motion and corrects member and wheel errors. For transfer from 0.10m to 0.075m, the method reduces objective-function evaluations by 63.7% relative to full recomputation. Contact-phase feasibility analysis covers step heights from 0.10 to 0.46m, or 1.08 to 4.97 wheel radii, with the upper value near the theoretical feasible boundary. The electric prototype traverses 2.11 wheel radii. The resulting low-dimensional representation stores task coordination in a hyper-redundant, load-bearing morphology and reuses it during locomotion.

</details>


### [154] [SwarmNxt: Open-source Software-Hardware Platform for Fast and Agile Aerial Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11382)
*Charbel Toumieh,Niel Mistry,Benjamin Jarvis,Simon Jeger,Peize Liu,Shaojie Shen,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个名为SwarmNxt的开源无人机群平台，支持室内多机自主导航、规避与感知，为飞行机器人集群实验提供完整工具链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用无人机平台多为闭源，且机载计算资源有限，不足以实现敏捷的集群自主导航，限制了安全、救援等领域的无人机群实际应用与研究发展。跨多平台开发与维护集群系统的工程成本高亟待解决。

Method: 本文基于开源OmniNxt无人机硬件，开发了SwarmNxt平台，涵盖硬件组装、软件自动化部署与维护、以及基于ROS 2的自主导航框架，集成了最新的控制、规划与深度估计算法，支持多无人机集群实验。

Result: 通过两个现实世界实验验证：一是六架无人机群实现去中心化规划与高速互避；二是四架无人机群在障碍环境下基于机载深度感知实现协同行为。所有主要算法均在机载端实时运行。

Conclusion: SwarmNxt降低了无人机集群研发门槛，为研究人员和工程师进行真实环境下的集群无人机实验提供了灵活、易用、可扩展的开源平台，有助于推动相关领域技术发展与应用落地。

Abstract: Aerial robot swarms have the potential to transform time-critical safety, security, and search-and-rescue operations. By coordinating multiple robots, they can rapidly survey disaster sites, map collapsed or GPS-denied environments, and search cluttered areas faster than a single robot, reducing response times and minimizing risks to first responders. Realizing this potential, however, requires robust autonomous swarm navigation, which remains an active research challenge. Progress is further constrained by existing platforms, as commercial drones are often closed-source or lack the onboard computational resources needed for agile, vision-based collective flight. Moreover, developing, deploying, and maintaining software across multiple aerial robots requires significant engineering effort. To address these challenges, we present SwarmNxt, an open-source software platform built on the open-source OmniNxt drone hardware. SwarmNxt provides an end-to-end toolkit, including detailed hardware assembly instructions with a video tutorial, automation tools for parallel software deployment and swarm-wide updates, and a ROS 2-based framework for autonomous navigation. The platform integrates state-of-the-art control, planning, and depth estimation into a single ROS 2 multi-agent system, providing an open research infrastructure for physical swarm experimentation. We validate SwarmNxt through two real-world experiments: a six-drone swarm performing decentralized planning with high-speed inter-drone collision avoidance, and a four-drone swarm executing collective flight with onboard depth estimation in an obstacle-filled environment. Both experiments were run indoors with global position from external motion capture; perception, planning, and control run onboard.

</details>


### [155] [Safety-aware Skill Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11433)
*A K M Nadimul Haque,Sheila Sutjipto,Marc G. Carmichael,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Dist-GPRL的距离感知与安全引导强化学习框架用于机器人技能自适应，相较于现有方法在动态和严峻环境下表现出更高的任务成功率、更低碰撞率和更稳定的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习技能自适应框架为保证稳定性，常需限制观测或控制探索，但在复杂动态环境下容易产生不安全行为和学习不稳定，尤其当与障碍物或移动目标接近时。解决这一问题，对安全和稳定性的要求尤为迫切。
